Title: Compendio storico di memorie cronologiche concernenti la religione e la morale della nazione armena suddita dell'Impero Ottomano; Tomo secondo
Creator: Giovanni de Serpos
Publisher: Venezia: nella stamperia di Carlo Palese, con pubblica approvazione
Publication Date: 1786
Rights: Public Domain (published 1786, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item compendiostoric02serpgoog (https://archive.org/details/compendiostoric02serpgoog), the item's own compendiostoric02serpgoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Oxford University; host metadata date: 1786. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 07f95b8bf64e79ecee02c4b742f6caf29f03c8ed9a262b77824020f3d8cae9cd.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at|http: //books' removed (46 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google scan of the Oxford copy; the Google notice removed. A second copy of this volume (compendiostoric03serpgoog, New York Public Library) was not used.
Content note: The second volume, continuing the history through the patriarchs and councils of the Armenian church in the fourth and later books, still argued toward the author's thesis on the nation's faith.
